# Additive screening of 2026-10-18, scenario 0

## Run identity

```yaml
model: claude-sonnet-5
served_version: claude-sonnet-5
tree: sha256:7d84079327a6496ad99d1047ec2c9d0f82a810c3765b0ae22a4ae3ea97be60e8
lock: sha256:1fb804541b7441a6588f57cc93c893118896dbf705119c83b1f9f77072386124
selection: sha256:0e2113cd81af83407d78a1964326c730f04457f9a81c929f9e32bfc73585bda3
read_set: sha256:30284342a7371374ee5ded1720314683046f3f9f7b372ecbb70ff7e6f3e8b5d3
seed: 0
harness: 0.6.0
tier: additive
arm: scenario-0
at: 2026-10-18
cost_cents: 6232
```

**This line is one of the screening round of the additive campaign that [#1775](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1775) asked for.** `tools/probe/campaign.sh` recorded it as line 1 of the plan of the batch, a plan that draws its 15 lines from `tools/probe/additive-screening.spec`. It is the `scenario-0` arm of the additive tier over 4 probes of the tuning set, at 18 repetitions per probe, on `claude-sonnet-5` under Claude Code 2.1.288 with a cap of 80 turns for each session.

**One tree, one pin, one attempt.** Every session ran in a fresh copy of a `git archive` of the pinned tree, and the engine of that tree. The pin was `2d212a26` for the whole batch, and the batch did not restart. The `tree` digest in the identity above is the digest the assembly recorded.

**What the recorder counted.** The line holds 72 sessions and cost 6232 cents: the intent hook live in 0, 0 stopped at the turn cap, 0 stopped at the session budget, 92 paths outside the workspace named, 0 sessions uncounted, 0 web-tool calls. A session the recorder could not count adds nothing to either count, so each count is a floor.

**What the halts spent.** The usage limit of the account halted the batch more than once. Before a halt stopped it, an attempt at 3 of its sessions spent 218 cents, and the driver then ran each of those sessions again from the start. The cost above counts only the attempt that recorded, so this line spent 218 cents more than it states, and the batch total over every attempt is in [#1775](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1775).

**The contract of this batch.** The sessions ran under the recorder contract of spec 15: a `bwrap` sandbox with no network of its own, a configuration directory that holds a copy of the credentials and nothing else, the permission mode `dontAsk`, and `WebSearch` and `WebFetch` denied. The round approved $700 over every attempt, and the driver ran with a cap of 70000 cents and a session budget of 300 cents.

**Where the raw recordings are.** The raw session logs are outside git, under `/mnt/build/hw-1775/screening/a/sessions/L1-*/raw.jsonl` on the host that ran the batch. Nobody deletes them until the report of [#1779](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1779) is published.

**Where the figures are.** The rates, the intervals and the comparisons of this line are in the generated result that reads this transcript, and this document states none of them.
